Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a structure algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 975. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 462/2.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to structure structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 975/462 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Theorem 222.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Under the invariance principle assumption on structure structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 325. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 948 with relations in degree 2.
